It NEVER relaxes the @Roles\n196\t// floor (SEC-MEDIUM-050) — both the role gate AND the feature gate apply.\n197\t@UseGuards(TenantGuard, MobileFeatureGuard)\n198\texport class HarvestResolver {\n199\t  private readonly logger = new Logger(HarvestResolver.name);\n200\t\n201\t  constructor(\n202\t    private readonly commandBus: CommandBus,\n203\t    private readonly queryBus: QueryBus,\n204\t  ) {}\n205\t\n206\t  // ==========================================================================\n207\t  // QUERIES\n208\t  // ==========================================================================\n209\t\n210\t  /**\n211\t   * List all harvest records with filtering and pagination\n212\t   */\n213\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER, Role.MODULE_USER)\n214\t  @Query(() => PaginatedHarvestsResponse, { description: 'List harvest records with filtering and pagination' })\n215\t  async harvests(\n216\t    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n217\t    @Args('filter', { type: () => HarvestFilterInput, nullable: true }) filter?: HarvestFilterInput,\n218\t    @Args('pagination', { type: () => HarvestPaginationInput, nullable: true }) pagination?: HarvestPaginationInput,\n219\t  ): Promise<IStandardPaginatedResult<HarvestRecord>> {\n220\t    this.logger.log(`Listing harvests for tenant ${tenantId}`);\n221\t\n222\t    const query = new ListHarvestsQuery(\n223\t      tenantId,\n224\t      filter ? {\n225\t        batchId: filter.batchId,\n226\t        batchIds: filter.batchIds,\n227\t        tankId: filter.tankId,\n228\t        tankIds: filter.tankIds,\n229\t        pondId: filter.pondId,\n230\t        siteId: filter.siteId,\n231\t        status: filter.status,\n232\t        statuses: filter.statuses,\n233\t        qualityClass: filter.qualityClass,\n234\t        qualityClasses: filter.qualityClasses,\n235\t        qualityGrade: filter.qualityGrade,\n236\t        qualityGrades: filter.qualityGrades,\n237\t        method: filter.method,\n238\t        productForm: filter.productForm,\n239\t        startDate: filter.startDate,\n240\t        endDate: filter.endDate,\n241\t        qualityApproved: filter.qualityApproved,\n242\t        harvestedBy: filter.harvestedBy,\n243\t        search: filter.search,\n244\t        minBiomass: filter.minBiomass,\n245\t        maxBiomass: filter.maxBiomass,\n246\t        minAverageWeight: filter.minAverageWeight,\n247\t        maxAverageWeight: filter.maxAverageWeight,\n248\t        minQuantity: filter.minQuantity,\n249\t        maxQuantity: filter.maxQuantity,\n250\t      } : undefined,\n251\t      pagination ? {\n252\t        page: pagination.page,\n253\t        limit: pagination.limit,\n254\t        sortBy: pagination.sortBy,\n255\t        sortOrder: pagination.sortOrder,\n256\t      } : undefined,\n257\t    );\n258\t\n259\t    const result = await this.queryBus.execute<ListHarvestsQuery, PaginatedQueryResult<HarvestRecord>>(query);\n260\t    return fromCqrsPaginated(result);\n261\t  }\n262\t\n263\t  /**\n264\t   * Get a single harvest record by ID\n265\t   */\n266\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER, Role.MODULE_USER)\n267\t  @Query(() => HarvestRecord, { nullable: true, description: 'Get a single harvest record by ID' })\n268\t  async harvest(\n269\t    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n270\t    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n271\t  ): Promise<HarvestRecord | null> {\n272\t    this.logger.log(`Getting harvest ${id} for tenant ${tenantId}`);\n273\t\n274\t    const query = new GetHarvestQuery(tenantId, id);\n275\t    return this.queryBus.execute(query);\n276\t  }\n277\t\n278\t  /**\n279\t   * Get harvest records for a specific batch\n280\t   */\n281\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER, Role.MODULE_USER)\n282\t  @Query(() => PaginatedHarvestsResponse, { description: 'Get harvest records for a specific batch' })\n283\t  async harvestsByBatch(\n284\t    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n285\t    @Args('batchId', { type: () => ID }) batchId: string,\n286\t    @Args('pagination', { type: () => HarvestPaginationInput, nullable: true }) pagination?: HarvestPaginationInput,\n287\t  ): Promise<IStandardPaginatedResult<HarvestRecord>> {\n288\t    this.logger.log(`Listing harvests for batch ${batchId} in tenant ${tenantId}`);\n289\t\n290\t    const query = new ListHarvestsQuery(\n291\t      tenantId,\n292\t      { batchId },\n293\t      pagination ? {\n294\t        page: pagination.page,\n295\t        limit: pagination.limit,\n296\t        sortBy: pagination.sortBy || 'harvestDate',\n297\t        sortOrder: pagination.sortOrder || 'DESC',\n298\t      } : { sortBy: 'harvestDate', sortOrder: 'DESC' },\n299\t    );\n300\t\n301\t    const result = await this.queryBus.execute<ListHarvestsQuery, PaginatedQueryResult<HarvestRecord>>(query);\n302\t    return fromCqrsPaginated(result);\n303\t  }\n304\t\n305\t  /**\n306\t   * Get harvest statistics for a tenant within a date range\n307\t   */\n308\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n309\t  @Query(() => HarvestStatisticsResponse, { description: 'Get harvest statistics for a tenant within a date range' })\n310\t  async harvestStatistics(\n311\t    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n312\t    @Args('dateRange', { type: () => DateRangeInput }) dateRange: DateRangeInput,\n313\t  ): Promise<HarvestStatisticsResponse> {\n314\t    this.logger.log(\n315\t      `Getting harvest statistics for tenant ${tenantId} from ` +\n316\t        `${dateRange.startDate.toISOString()} to ${dateRange.endDate.toISOString()}`,\n317\t    );\n318\t\n319\t    const query = new GetHarvestStatisticsQuery(tenantId, {\n320\t      startDate: dateRange.startDate,\n321\t      endDate: dateRange.endDate,\n322\t    });\n323\t\n324\t    const result: HarvestStatistics = await this.queryBus.execute(query);\n325\t\n326\t    return {\n327\t      tenantId: result.tenantId,\n328\t      startDate: result.dateRange.startDate,\n329\t      endDate: result.dateRange.endDate,\n330\t      summary: result.summary,\n331\t      byStatus: result.byStatus,\n332\t      byQualityGrade: result.byQualityGrade,\n333\t      byMonth: result.byMonth,\n334\t      trends: result.trends,\n335\t    };\n336\t  }\n337\t\n338\t  // ==========================================================================\n339\t  // MUTATIONS\n340\t  // ==========================================================================\n341\t\n342\t  /**\n343\t   * Create a new harvest record\n344\t   */\n345\t  // Return HarvestRecord (not Batch) so the frontend receives harvest-specific fields.\n346\t  // SEC-MEDIUM-050: the role floor is the SSoT — createHarvestRecord stays\n347\t  // MODULE_MANAGER+ (NO MODULE_USER). The mobile 'harvest' feature gate below\n348\t  // NEVER widens it; both gates apply.\n349\t  // Keep this comment ABOVE @Mutation: the farm-graphql-fe-be-parity extractor\n350\t  // skips interleaved DECORATORS but not comments, so a comment between\n351\t  // @Mutation and the method hides the field from the FE↔BE parity scan.\n352\t  @Mutation(() => HarvestRecord, { description: 'Create a harvest record and update batch/tank quantities' })\n353\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n354\t  @RequiresMobileFeature('harvest')\n355\t  async createHarvestRecord(\n356\t    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n357\t    @CurrentUser() user: UserContext,\n358\t    @Args('input') input: CreateHarvestRecordInput,\n359\t  ): Promise<HarvestRecord> {\n360\t    this.logger.log(`Creating harvest record for tenant ${tenantId} by user ${user.sub}`);\n361\t\n362\t    return this.commandBus.execute(\n363\t      new CreateHarvestRecordCommand(\n364\t        tenantId,\n365\t        input,\n366\t        user.sub,\n367\t        user.roles,\n368\t        user.assignedSiteIds ?? [],\n369\t        mobileCommandEnvelopeFromInput(input),\n370\t      ),\n371\t    );\n372\t  }\n373\t\n374\t  /**\n375\t   * Update an existing harvest record\n376\t   */\n377\t  @Mutation(() => HarvestRecord, { description: 'Update an existing harvest record' })\n378\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n379\t  async updateHarvestRecord(\n380\t    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n381\t    @CurrentUser() user: UserContext,\n382\t    @Args('input') input: UpdateHarvestRecordInput,\n383\t  ): Promise<HarvestRecord> {\n384\t    this.logger.log(`Updating harvest record ${input.id} for tenant ${tenantId} by user ${user.sub}`);\n385\t\n386\t    return this.commandBus.execute(\n387\t      new UpdateHarvestRecordCommand(\n388\t        tenantId,\n389\t        input.id,\n390\t        {\n391\t          status: input.status,\n392\t          qualityClass: input.qualityClass,\n393\t          method: input.method,\n394\t          productForm: input.productForm,\n395\t          totalRevenue: input.totalRevenue,\n396\t          harvestCost: input.harvestCost,\n397\t          currency: input.currency,\n398\t          mortalityDuringHarvest: input.mortalityDuringHarvest,\n399\t          rejectedQuantity: input.rejectedQuantity,\n400\t          rejectionReason: input.rejectionReason,\n401\t          notes: input.notes,\n402\t        },\n403\t        user.sub\n404\t      )\n405\t    );\n406\t  }\n407\t\n408\t  /**\n409\t   * Delete (soft delete) a harvest record\n410\t   */\n411\t  @Mutation(() => Boolean, { description: 'Delete (cancel) a harvest record and reverse quantity changes' })\n412\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n413\t  async deleteHarvestRecord(\n414\t    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n415\t    @CurrentUser() user: UserContext,\n416\t    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n417\t  ): Promise<boolean> {\n418\t    this.logger.log(`Deleting harvest record ${id} for tenant ${tenantId} by user ${user.sub}`);\n419\t\n420\t    return this.commandBus.execute(\n421\t      new DeleteHarvestRecordCommand(tenantId, id, user.sub)\n422\t    );\n423\t  }\n424\t\n425\t  // ==========================================================================\n426\t  // FIELD RESOLVERS\n427\t  // ==========================================================================\n428\t\n429\t  /**\n430\t   * Calculate net yield (after rejected quantity)\n431\t   */\n432\t  @ResolveField(() => Float)\n433\t  netBiomass(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number {\n434\t    const rejected = Number(harvest.rejectedQuantity || 0);\n435\t    return Number(harvest.totalBiomass) - rejected;\n436\t  }\n437\t\n438\t  /**\n439\t   * Calculate price per kg\n440\t   */\n441\t  @ResolveField(() => Float, { nullable: true })\n442\t  pricePerKg(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number | null {\n443\t    if (!harvest.totalRevenue || !harvest.totalBiomass) return null;\n444\t    return Number(harvest.totalRevenue) / Number(harvest.totalBiomass);\n445\t  }\n446\t\n447\t  /**\n448\t   * Calculate profit margin\n449\t   */\n450\t  @ResolveField(() => Float, { nullable: true })\n451\t  profitMargin(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number | null {\n452\t    if (!harvest.totalRevenue || !harvest.harvestCost) return null;\n453\t    const revenue = Number(harvest.totalRevenue);\n454\t    const cost = Number(harvest.harvestCost);\n455\t    if (revenue === 0) return null;\n456\t    return ((revenue - cost) / revenue) * 100;\n457\t  }\n458\t\n459\t  /**\n460\t   * Check if harvest is complete\n461\t   */\n462\t  @ResolveField(() => Boolean)\n463\t  isComplete(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): boolean {\n464\t    return harvest.status === HarvestRecordStatus.COMPLETED ||\n465\t           harvest.status === HarvestRecordStatus.DISPATCHED ||\n466\t           harvest.status === HarvestRecordStatus.DELIVERED;\n467\t  }\n468\t\n469\t  /**\n470\t   * Check if harvest can be edited\n471\t   */\n472\t  @ResolveField(() => Boolean)\n473\t  canEdit(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): boolean {\n474\t    return harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.DISPATCHED &&\n475\t           harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.DELIVERED &&\n476\t           harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.CANCELLED;\n477\t  }\n478\t\n479\t  /**\n480\t   * Check if harvest can be deleted\n481\t   */\n482\t  @ResolveField(() => Boolean)\n483\t  canDelete(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): boolean {\n484\t    return harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.DISPATCHED &&\n485\t           harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.DELIVERED &&\n486\t           harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.CANCELLED;\n487\t  }\n488\t\n489\t  /**\n490\t   * Get rejection percentage\n491\t   */\n492\t  @ResolveField(() => Float)\n493\t  rejectionPercentage(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number {\n494\t    if (!harvest.rejectedQuantity || !harvest.totalBiomass) return 0;\n495\t    return (Number(harvest.rejectedQuantity) / Number(harvest.totalBiomass)) * 100;\n496\t  }\n497\t\n498\t  /**\n499\t   * Get mortality percentage during harvest\n500\t   */\n501\t  @ResolveField(() => Float)\n502\t  harvestMortalityPercentage(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number {\n503\t    if (!harvest.mortalityDuringHarvest || !harvest.quantityHarvested) return 0;\n504\t    const totalAttempted = harvest.quantityHarvested + harvest.mortalityDuringHarvest;\n505\t    return (harvest.mortalityDuringHarvest / totalAttempted) * 100;\n506\t  }\n507\t}\n508\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9cdaf3f8-2b4f-4f3f-95af-eef580e7197a","uuid":"d2abbd18-d40f-47c4-8366-f2a8d60369c4","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:05:51.236Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-b9d9a4f17928/apps/farm-service/src/harvest/resolvers/harvest.resolver.ts","content":"// RESOLVER\n// ============================================================================\n\n@Resolver(() => HarvestRecord)\n// SEC-HIGH-052: MobileFeatureGuard enforces the 'harvest' entitlement on\n// createHarvestRecord (no-op on other routes). It NEVER relaxes the @Roles\n// floor (SEC-MEDIUM-050) — both the role gate AND the feature gate apply.\n@UseGuards(TenantGuard, MobileFeatureGuard)\nexport class HarvestResolver {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(HarvestResolver.name);\n\n  constructor(\n    private readonly commandBus: CommandBus,\n    private readonly queryBus: QueryBus,\n  ) {}\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // QUERIES\n  // ==========================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * List all harvest records with filtering and pagination\n   */\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER, Role.MODULE_USER)\n  @Query(() => PaginatedHarvestsResponse, { description: 'List harvest records with filtering and pagination' })\n  async harvests(\n    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n    @Args('filter', { type: () => HarvestFilterInput, nullable: true }) filter?: HarvestFilterInput,\n    @Args('pagination', { type: () => HarvestPaginationInput, nullable: true }) pagination?: HarvestPaginationInput,\n  ): Promise<IStandardPaginatedResult<HarvestRecord>> {\n    this.logger.log(`Listing harvests for tenant ${tenantId}`);\n\n    const query = new ListHarvestsQuery(\n      tenantId,\n      filter ? {\n        batchId: filter.batchId,\n        batchIds: filter.batchIds,\n        tankId: filter.tankId,\n        tankIds: filter.tankIds,\n        pondId: filter.pondId,\n        siteId: filter.siteId,\n        status: filter.status,\n        statuses: filter.statuses,\n        qualityClass: filter.qualityClass,\n        qualityClasses: filter.qualityClasses,\n        qualityGrade: filter.qualityGrade,\n        qualityGrades: filter.qualityGrades,\n        method: filter.method,\n        productForm: filter.productForm,\n        startDate: filter.startDate,\n        endDate: filter.endDate,\n        qualityApproved: filter.qualityApproved,\n        harvestedBy: filter.harvestedBy,\n        search: filter.search,\n        minBiomass: filter.minBiomass,\n        maxBiomass: filter.maxBiomass,\n        minAverageWeight: filter.minAverageWeight,\n        maxAverageWeight: filter.maxAverageWeight,\n        minQuantity: filter.minQuantity,\n        maxQuantity: filter.maxQuantity,\n      } : undefined,\n      pagination ? {\n        page: pagination.page,\n        limit: pagination.limit,\n        sortBy: pagination.sortBy,\n        sortOrder: pagination.sortOrder,\n      } : undefined,\n    );\n\n    const result = await this.queryBus.execute<ListHarvestsQuery, PaginatedQueryResult<HarvestRecord>>(query);\n    return fromCqrsPaginated(result);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get a single harvest record by ID\n   */\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER, Role.MODULE_USER)\n  @Query(() => HarvestRecord, { nullable: true, description: 'Get a single harvest record by ID' })\n  async harvest(\n    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n  ): Promise<HarvestRecord | null> {\n    this.logger.log(`Getting harvest ${id} for tenant ${tenantId}`);\n\n    const query = new GetHarvestQuery(tenantId, id);\n    return this.queryBus.execute(query);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get harvest records for a specific batch\n   */\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER, Role.MODULE_USER)\n  @Query(() => PaginatedHarvestsResponse, { description: 'Get harvest records for a specific batch' })\n  async harvestsByBatch(\n    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n    @Args('batchId', { type: () => ID }) batchId: string,\n    @Args('pagination', { type: () => HarvestPaginationInput, nullable: true }) pagination?: HarvestPaginationInput,\n  ): Promise<IStandardPaginatedResult<HarvestRecord>> {\n    this.logger.log(`Listing harvests for batch ${batchId} in tenant ${tenantId}`);\n\n    const query = new ListHarvestsQuery(\n      tenantId,\n      { batchId },\n      pagination ? {\n        page: pagination.page,\n        limit: pagination.limit,\n        sortBy: pagination.sortBy || 'harvestDate',\n        sortOrder: pagination.sortOrder || 'DESC',\n      } : { sortBy: 'harvestDate', sortOrder: 'DESC' },\n    );\n\n    const result = await this.queryBus.execute<ListHarvestsQuery, PaginatedQueryResult<HarvestRecord>>(query);\n    return fromCqrsPaginated(result);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get harvest statistics for a tenant within a date range\n   */\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  @Query(() => HarvestStatisticsResponse, { description: 'Get harvest statistics for a tenant within a date range' })\n  async harvestStatistics(\n    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n    @Args('dateRange', { type: () => DateRangeInput }) dateRange: DateRangeInput,\n  ): Promise<HarvestStatisticsResponse> {\n    this.logger.log(\n      `Getting harvest statistics for tenant ${tenantId} from ` +\n        `${dateRange.startDate.toISOString()} to ${dateRange.endDate.toISOString()}`,\n    );\n\n    const query = new GetHarvestStatisticsQuery(tenantId, {\n      startDate: dateRange.startDate,\n      endDate: dateRange.endDate,\n    });\n\n    const result: HarvestStatistics = await this.queryBus.execute(query);\n\n    return {\n      tenantId: result.tenantId,\n      startDate: result.dateRange.startDate,\n      endDate: result.dateRange.endDate,\n      summary: result.summary,\n      byStatus: result.byStatus,\n      byQualityGrade: result.byQualityGrade,\n      byMonth: result.byMonth,\n      trends: result.trends,\n    };\n  }\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // MUTATIONS\n  // ==========================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * Create a new harvest record\n   */\n  // Return HarvestRecord (not Batch) so the frontend receives harvest-specific fields.\n  // SEC-MEDIUM-050: the role floor is the SSoT — createHarvestRecord stays\n  // MODULE_MANAGER+ (NO MODULE_USER). The mobile 'harvest' feature gate below\n  // NEVER widens it; both gates apply.\n  // Keep this comment ABOVE @Mutation: the farm-graphql-fe-be-parity extractor\n  // skips interleaved DECORATORS but not comments, so a comment between\n  // @Mutation and the method hides the field from the FE↔BE parity scan.\n  @Mutation(() => HarvestRecord, { description: 'Create a harvest record and update batch/tank quantities' })\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  @RequiresMobileFeature('harvest')\n  async createHarvestRecord(\n    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n    @CurrentUser() user: UserContext,\n    @Args('input') input: CreateHarvestRecordInput,\n  ): Promise<HarvestRecord> {\n    this.logger.log(`Creating harvest record for tenant ${tenantId} by user ${user.sub}`);\n\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new CreateHarvestRecordCommand(\n        tenantId,\n        input,\n        user.sub,\n        user.roles,\n        user.assignedSiteIds ?? [],\n        mobileCommandEnvelopeFromInput(input),\n      ),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Update an existing harvest record\n   */\n  @Mutation(() => HarvestRecord, { description: 'Update an existing harvest record' })\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  async updateHarvestRecord(\n    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n    @CurrentUser() user: UserContext,\n    @Args('input') input: UpdateHarvestRecordInput,\n  ): Promise<HarvestRecord> {\n    this.logger.log(`Updating harvest record ${input.id} for tenant ${tenantId} by user ${user.sub}`);\n\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new UpdateHarvestRecordCommand(\n        tenantId,\n        input.id,\n        {\n          status: input.status,\n          qualityClass: input.qualityClass,\n          method: input.method,\n          productForm: input.productForm,\n          totalRevenue: input.totalRevenue,\n          harvestCost: input.harvestCost,\n          currency: input.currency,\n          mortalityDuringHarvest: input.mortalityDuringHarvest,\n          rejectedQuantity: input.rejectedQuantity,\n          rejectionReason: input.rejectionReason,\n          notes: input.notes,\n        },\n        user.sub\n      )\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Delete (soft delete) a harvest record\n   */\n  @Mutation(() => Boolean, { description: 'Delete (cancel) a harvest record and reverse quantity changes' })\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  async deleteHarvestRecord(\n    @Tenant() tenantId: string,\n    @CurrentUser() user: UserContext,\n    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n  ): Promise<boolean> {\n    this.logger.log(`Deleting harvest record ${id} for tenant ${tenantId} by user ${user.sub}`);\n\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new DeleteHarvestRecordCommand(tenantId, id, user.sub)\n    );\n  }\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // FIELD RESOLVERS\n  // ==========================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * Calculate net yield (after rejected quantity)\n   */\n  @ResolveField(() => Float)\n  netBiomass(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number {\n    const rejected = Number(harvest.rejectedQuantity || 0);\n    return Number(harvest.totalBiomass) - rejected;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Calculate price per kg\n   */\n  @ResolveField(() => Float, { nullable: true })\n  pricePerKg(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number | null {\n    if (!harvest.totalRevenue || !harvest.totalBiomass) return null;\n    return Number(harvest.totalRevenue) / Number(harvest.totalBiomass);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Calculate profit margin\n   */\n  @ResolveField(() => Float, { nullable: true })\n  profitMargin(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number | null {\n    if (!harvest.totalRevenue || !harvest.harvestCost) return null;\n    const revenue = Number(harvest.totalRevenue);\n    const cost = Number(harvest.harvestCost);\n    if (revenue === 0) return null;\n    return ((revenue - cost) / revenue) * 100;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if harvest is complete\n   */\n  @ResolveField(() => Boolean)\n  isComplete(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): boolean {\n    return harvest.status === HarvestRecordStatus.COMPLETED ||\n           harvest.status === HarvestRecordStatus.DISPATCHED ||\n           harvest.status === HarvestRecordStatus.DELIVERED;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if harvest can be edited\n   */\n  @ResolveField(() => Boolean)\n  canEdit(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): boolean {\n    return harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.DISPATCHED &&\n           harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.DELIVERED &&\n           harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.CANCELLED;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Check if harvest can be deleted\n   */\n  @ResolveField(() => Boolean)\n  canDelete(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): boolean {\n    return harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.DISPATCHED &&\n           harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.DELIVERED &&\n           harvest.status !== HarvestRecordStatus.CANCELLED;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get rejection percentage\n   */\n  @ResolveField(() => Float)\n  rejectionPercentage(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number {\n    if (!harvest.rejectedQuantity || !harvest.totalBiomass) return 0;\n    return (Number(harvest.rejectedQuantity) / Number(harvest.totalBiomass)) * 100;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get mortality percentage during harvest\n   */\n  @ResolveField(() => Float)\n  harvestMortalityPercentage(@Parent() harvest: HarvestRecord): number {\n    if (!harvest.mortalityDuringHarvest || !harvest.quantityHarvested) return 0;\n    const totalAttempted = harvest.quantityHarvested + harvest.mortalityDuringHarvest;\n    return (harvest.mortalityDuringHarvest / totalAttempted) * 100;\n  }\n}\n","numLines":319,"startLine":190,"totalLines":508}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01F2nzzuV9tUgo8wuDVHK7wQ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Permission Matrix Invariant Test\n3\t *\n4\t * Scans every `*.resolver.ts` in apps/farm-service/src and asserts\n5\t * the @Roles / @Mutation / @Query decorator state agrees with\n6\t * `permission-matrix.ts`. Three failure modes:\n7\t *\n8\t *   1. A @Mutation / @Query is declared but missing from BOTH the\n9\t *      role matrix AND the ungated grandfather set → fail. Every\n10\t *      new operation must be classified deliberately.\n11\t *\n12\t *   2. An operation appears in the matrix but its @Roles in source\n13\t *      differs from the declared roles → fail. The matrix is the\n14\t *      authoritative intent; drift in source without a matrix\n15\t *      update is treated as an unreviewed authorisation change.\n16\t *\n17\t *   3. An operation appears in the grandfather whitelist but\n18\t *      source DOES carry a @Roles decorator → fail. The operation\n19\t *      has graduated — move it out of UNGATED_OPERATIONS and into\n20\t *      MUTATION_ROLES / QUERY_ROLES.\n21\t *\n22\t * The invariant intentionally does NOT police the ungated\n23\t * whitelist shrinking — phase 6.1.1 reduces it module by module.\n24\t *\n25\t * Phase 6.1 of the \"Farm modülü kalan kör noktalar\" plan.\n26\t */\n27\timport { resolve } from 'path';\n28\t\n29\timport {\n30\t  MUTATION_ROLES,\n31\t  QUERY_ROLES,\n32\t  UNGATED_OPERATIONS,\n33\t} from '../permission-matrix';\n34\timport { scanResolvers, ResolverOperation } from '../resolver-scanner';\n35\t\n36\tconst FARM_SERVICE_SRC = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..');\n37\t\n38\tfunction rolesToSortedNames(roles: readonly string[]): string[] {\n39\t  return [...new Set(roles)].sort();\n40\t}\n41\t\n42\tfunction matrixEntryNames(\n43\t  matrix: Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>>,\n44\t  operation: string,\n45\t): string[] {\n46\t  const entry = matrix[operation];\n47\t  if (!entry) return [];\n48\t  return rolesToSortedNames(entry.map((r) => String(r)));\n49\t}\n50\t\n51\tdescribe('Farm-service permission matrix invariants', () => {\n52\t  let operations: ResolverOperation[];\n53\t  let mutationOps: ResolverOperation[];\n54\t  let queryOps: ResolverOperation[];\n55\t\n56\t  beforeAll(() => {\n57\t    operations = scanResolvers(FARM_SERVICE_SRC);\n58\t    mutationOps = operations.filter((o) => o.kind === 'Mutation');\n59\t    queryOps = operations.filter((o) => o.kind === 'Query');\n60\t  });\n61\t\n62\t  it('discovers a reasonable number of operations (sanity check)', () => {\n63\t    // Drops below this floor = scanner regex drift.\n64\t    expect(mutationOps.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(150);\n65\t    expect(queryOps.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(150);\n66\t  });\n67\t\n68\t  it('every @Mutation is classified (either role-listed or grandfathered)', () => {\n69\t    const classified = new Set<string>([\n70\t      ...Object.keys(MUTATION_ROLES),\n71\t      ...UNGATED_OPERATIONS,\n72\t    ]);\n73\t    const unclassified = Array.from(\n74\t      new Set(\n75\t        mutationOps\n76\t          .map((op) => op.operation)\n77\t          .filter((name) => !classified.has(name)),\n78\t      ),\n79\t    ).sort();\n80\t    expect(unclassified).toEqual([]);\n81\t  });\n82\t\n83\t  it('every @Query is classified', () => {\n84\t    const classified = new Set<string>([\n85\t      ...Object.keys(QUERY_ROLES),\n86\t      ...UNGATED_OPERATIONS,\n87\t    ]);\n88\t    const unclassified = Array.from(\n89\t      new Set(\n90\t        queryOps\n91\t          .map((op) => op.operation)\n92\t          .filter((name) => !classified.has(name)),\n93\t      ),\n94\t    ).sort();\n95\t    expect(unclassified).toEqual([]);\n96\t  });\n97\t\n98\t  it('MUTATION_ROLES entries match the @Roles decorators in source', () => {\n99\t    const mismatches: Array<{\n100\t      operation: string;\n101\t      matrix: string[];\n102\t      source: string[];\n103\t    }> = [];\n104\t    for (const [operation, roles] of Object.entries(MUTATION_ROLES)) {\n105\t      const matches = mutationOps.filter((op) => op.operation === operation);\n106\t      if (matches.length === 0) continue; // orphan matrix entry caught below\n107\t      const matrixRoles = rolesToSortedNames(roles.map((r) => String(r)));\n108\t      for (const src of matches) {\n109\t        const sourceRoles = rolesToSortedNames(src.roles);\n110\t        if (\n111\t          sourceRoles.length !== matrixRoles.length ||\n112\t          sourceRoles.some((r, i) => r !== matrixRoles[i])\n113\t        ) {\n114\t          mismatches.push({\n115\t            operation: `${operation} (${src.filePath}:${src.line})`,\n116\t            matrix: matrixRoles,\n117\t            source: sourceRoles,\n118\t          });\n119\t        }\n120\t      }\n121\t    }\n122\t    expect(mismatches).toEqual([]);\n123\t  });\n124\t\n125\t  it('QUERY_ROLES entries match the @Roles decorators in source', () => {\n126\t    const mismatches: Array<{\n127\t      operation: string;\n128\t      matrix: string[];\n129\t      source: string[];\n130\t    }> = [];\n131\t    for (const [operation, roles] of Object.entries(QUERY_ROLES)) {\n132\t      const matches = {\n155\t      const matches = operations.filter((op) => op.operation === ungated);\n156\t      for (const src of matches) {\n157\t        if (src.roles.length > 0) {\n158\t New operations added after this point either\n211\t    // carry @Roles + a matrix entry or the invariant test\n212\t    // rejects the PR — the fail-closed runtime guard (phase\n213\t    // 6.1.2) then rejects the request in production as a\n214\t    // defence-in-depth layer. No more grandfathering.\n215\t    expect(UNGATED_OPERATIONS.size).toBe(0);\n216\t  });\n217\t});\n218\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9cdaf3f8-2b4f-4f3f-95af-eef580e7197a","uuid":"cd22d7bb-201e-4377-b218-9d5203472355","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:06:39.168Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-b9d9a4f17928/apps/farm-service/src/common/authz/__tests__/permission-matrix.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Permission Matrix Invariant Test\n *\n * Scans every `*.resolver.ts` in apps/farm-service/src and asserts\n * the @Roles / @Mutation / @Query decorator state agrees with\n * `permission-matrix.ts`. Three failure modes:\n *\n *   1. A @Mutation / @Query is declared but missing from BOTH the\n *      role matrix AND the ungated grandfather set → fail. Every\n *      new operation must be classified deliberately.\n *\n *   2. An operation appears in the matrix but its @Roles in source\n *      differs from the declared roles → fail. The matrix is the\n *      authoritative intent; drift in source without a matrix\n *      update is treated as an unreviewed authorisation change.\n *\n *   3. An operation appears in the grandfather whitelist but\n *      source DOES carry a @Roles decorator → fail. The operation\n *      has graduated — move it out of UNGATED_OPERATIONS and into\n *      MUTATION_ROLES / QUERY_ROLES.\n *\n * The invariant intentionally does NOT police the ungated\n * whitelist shrinking — phase 6.1.1 reduces it module by module.\n *\n * Phase 6.1 of the \"Farm modülü kalan kör noktalar\" plan.\n */\nimport { resolve } from 'path';\n\nimport {\n  MUTATION_ROLES,\n  QUERY_ROLES,\n  UNGATED_OPERATIONS,\n} from '../permission-matrix';\nimport { scanResolvers, ResolverOperation } from '../resolver-scanner';\n\nconst FARM_SERVICE_SRC = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..');\n\nfunction rolesToSortedNames(roles: readonly string[]): string[] {\n  return [...new Set(roles)].sort();\n}\n\nfunction matrixEntryNames(\n  matrix: Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>>,\n  operation: string,\n): string[] {\n  const entry = matrix[operation];\n  if (!entry) return [];\n  return rolesToSortedNames(entry.map((r) => String(r)));\n}\n\ndescribe('Farm-service permission matrix invariants', () => {\n  let operations: ResolverOperation[];\n  let mutationOps: ResolverOperation[];\n  let queryOps: ResolverOperation[];\n\n  beforeAll(() => {\n    operations = scanResolvers(FARM_SERVICE_SRC);\n    mutationOps = operations.filter((o) => o.kind === 'Mutation');\n    queryOps = operations.filter((o) => o.kind === 'Query');\n  });\n\n  it('discovers a reasonable number of operations (sanity check)', () => {\n    // Drops below this floor = scanner regex drift.\n    expect(mutationOps.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(150);\n    expect(queryOps.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(150);\n  });\n\n  it('every @Mutation is classified (either role-listed or grandfathered)', () => {\n    const classified = new Set<string>([\n      ...Object.keys(MUTATION_ROLES),\n      ...UNGATED_OPERATIONS,\n    ]);\n    const unclassified = Array.from(\n      new Set(\n        mutationOps\n          .map((op) => op.operation)\n          .filter((name) => !classified.has(name)),\n      ),\n    ).sort();\n    expect(unclassified).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every @Query is classified', () => {\n    const classified = new Set<string>([\n      ...Object.keys(QUERY_ROLES),\n      ...UNGATED_OPERATIONS,\n    ]);\n    const unclassified = Array.from(\n      new Set(\n        queryOps\n          .map((op) => op.operation)\n          .filter((name) => !classified.has(name)),\n      ),\n    ).sort();\n    expect(unclassified).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('MUTATION_ROLES entries match the @Roles decorators in source', () => {\n    const mismatches: Array<{\n      operation: string;\n      matrix: string[];\n      source: string[];\n    }> = [];\n    for (const [operation, roles] of Object.entries(MUTATION_ROLES)) {\n      const matches = mutationOps.filter((op) => op.operation === operation);\n      if (matches.length === 0) continue; // orphan matrix entry caught below\n      const matrixRoles = rolesToSortedNames(roles.map((r) => String(r)));\n      for (const src of matches) {\n        const sourceRoles = rolesToSortedNames(src.roles);\n        if (\n          sourceRoles.length !== matrixRoles.length ||\n          sourceRoles.some((r, i) => r !== matrixRoles[i])\n        ) {\n          mismatches.push({\n            operation: `${operation} (${src.filePath}:${src.line})`,\n            matrix: matrixRoles,\n            source: sourceRoles,\n          });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    expect(mismatches).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('QUERY_ROLES entries match the @Roles decorators in source', () => {\n    const mismatches: Array<{\n      operation: string;\n      matrix: string[];\n      source: string[];\n    }> = [];\n    for (const [operation, roles] of Object.entries(QUERY_ROLES)) {\n      const matches = queryOps.filter((op) => op.operation === operation);\n      if (matches.length === 0) continue;\n      const matrixRoles = rolesToSortedNames(roles.map((r) => String(r)));\n      for (const src of matches) {\n        const sourceRoles = rolesToSortedNames(src.roles);\n        if (\n          sourceRoles.length !== matrixRoles.length ||\n          sourceRoles.some((r, i) => r !== matrixRoles[i])\n        ) {\n          mismatches.push({\n            operation: `${operation} (${src.filePath}:${src.line})`,\n            matrix: matrixRoles,\n            source: sourceRoles,\n          });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    expect(mismatches).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('grandfathered operations carry NO @Roles — opposite state means the operation has graduated', () => {\n    const graduated: string[] = [];\n    for (const ungated of UNGATED_OPERATIONS) {\n      const matches = operations.filter((op) => op.operation === ungated);\n      for (const src of matches) {\n        if (src.roles.length > 0) {\n          graduated.push(\n            `${src.operation} (${src.filePath}:${src.line}) now has @Roles — ` +\n              `move it out of UNGATED_OPERATIONS and into ` +\n              `${src.kind === 'Mutation' ? 'MUTATION_ROLES' : 'QUERY_ROLES'}.`,\n          );\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    expect(graduated).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every matrix key matches exactly one @Mutation / @Query in source (no stale entries)', () => {\n    const sourceMutations = new Set(mutationOps.map((op) => op.operation));\n    const sourceQueries = new Set(queryOps.map((op) => op.operation));\n\n    const staleMutations = Object.keys(MUTATION_ROLES).filter(\n      (op) => !sourceMutations.has(op),\n    );\n    const staleQueries = Object.keys(QUERY_ROLES).filter(\n      (op) => !sourceQueries.has(op),\n    );\n    const staleUngated = Array.from(UNGATED_OPERATIONS).filter(\n      (op) => !sourceMutations.has(op) && !sourceQueries.has(op),\n    );\n\n    expect({\n      staleMutations,\n      staleQueries,\n      staleUngated,\n    }).toEqual({\n      staleMutations: [],\n      staleQueries: [],\n      staleUngated: [],\n    });\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('resolveAllowedRoles helper', () => {\n  it('returns the role list for a known mutation', () => {\n    // Example chosen from MUTATION_ROLES snapshot — closeBatch is\n    // role-gated so the helper returns the exact array.\n    const closeBatchRoles = matrixEntryNames(\n      MUTATION_ROLES as unknown as Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>>,\n      'closeBatch',\n    );\n    expect(closeBatchRoles.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n  });\n\n  it('grandfathered whitelist is empty after phase 6.1.1 — every operation is explicitly classified', () => {\n    // Phase 6.1.1 is complete. Every @Mutation / @Query in\n    // farm-service now appears in MUTATION_ROLES or QUERY_ROLES\n    // with an explicit @Roles decorator. The whitelist is the\n    // empty set. New operations added after this point either\n    // carry @Roles + a matrix entry or the invariant test\n    // rejects the PR — the fail-closed runtime guard (phase\n    // 6.1.2) then rejects the request in production as a\n    // defence-in-depth layer. No more grandfathering.\n    expect(UNGATED_OPERATIONS.size).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":218,"startLine":1,"totalLines":218}}}

The regex is anchored on\n14\t * those conventions, and the invariant test fails loudly if the\n15\t * pattern ever drifts — which is the safest way to catch a bad\n16\t * decorator stack that might silently open up a mutation.\n17\t *\n18\t * Used by:\n19\t *   - `permission-matrix.ts` at design time to snapshot the intent\n20\t *     of the codebase into a single source of truth.\n21\t *   - `permission-matrix.spec.ts` invariant test to guarantee the\n22\t *     matrix stays in sync with the resolvers on every PR.\n23\t *\n24\t * Phase 6.1 of the \"Farm modülü kalan kör noktalar\" plan. Closes\n25\t * Girdi 15-C2.\n26\t */\n27\timport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'fs';\n28\timport { join, resolve } from 'path';\n29\t\n30\texport interface ResolverOperation {\n31\t  /** Top-level GraphQL operation name (method name by default). */\n32\t  operation: string;\n33\t  kind: 'Mutation' | 'Query';\n34\t  /** Bare role tokens as written in the decorator (without the Role. prefix). */\n35\t  roles: string[];\n36\t  /** Absolute file path so test output can cite the offending line. */\n37\t  filePath: string;\n38\t  /** Class member line number (1-based). */\n39\t  line: number;\n40\t}\n41\t\n42\tconst DEFAULT_FARM_SERVICE_ROOT = resolve(\n43\t  __dirname,\n44\t  '..',\n45\t  '..',\n46\t  '..',\n47\t);\n48\t\n49\t/**\n50\t * Produce the sorted list of every root-level @Mutation / @Query in\n51\t * the given farm-service source tree. When `rootDir` is omitted we\n52\t * walk the tree relative to this file's compiled location, which\n53\t * resolves to `apps/farm-service/src` in both dev and Jest runs.\n54\t */\n55\texport function scanResolvers(rootDir: string = DEFAULT_FARM_SERVICE_ROOT): ResolverOperation[] {\n56\t  const resolverFiles = collectResolverFiles(rootDir);\n57\t  const ops: ResolverOperation[] = [];\n58\t  for (const file of resolverFiles) {\n59\t    ops.push(...extractOperationsFromFile(file));\n60\t  }\n61\t  ops.sort((a, b) => {\n62\t    if (a.kind !== b.kind) return a.kind === 'Mutation' ? -1 : 1;\n63\t    return a.operation.localeCompare(b.operation);\n64\t  });\n65\t  return ops;\n66\t}\n67\t\n68\tfunction collectResolverFiles(dir: string, acc: string[] = []): string[] {\n69\t  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {\n70\t    const full = join(dir, entry);\n71\t    const stat = statSync(full);\n72\t    if (stat.isDirectory()) {\n73\t      if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === '__tests__' || entry === 'dist') {\n74\t        continue;\n75\t      }\n76\t      collectResolverFiles(full, acc);\n77\t      continue;\n78\t    }\n79\t    if (stat.isFile() && entry.endsWith('.resolver.ts') && !entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) {\n80\t      acc.push(full);\n81\t    }\n82\t  }\n83\t  return acc;\n84\t}\n85\t\n86\tconst ROLES_LINE_RE =\n87\t  /^\\s*@Roles\\(\\s*([^)]*)\\)\\s*$/;\n88\tconst MUTATION_OR_QUERY_LINE_RE =\n89\t  /^\\s*@(Mutation|Query)\\(/;\n90\tconst METHOD_SIG_RE =\n91\t  /^\\s*(?:public\\s+|protected\\s+|private\\s+)?(?:async\\s+)?([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\s*[(<]/;\n92\tconst ROLE_TOKEN_RE = /Role\\.([A-Z_]+)/g;\n93\tconst NAME_OPTION_RE =\n94\t  /name\\s*:\\s*['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]/;\n95\t\n96\tfunction extractOperationsFromFile(file: string): ResolverOperation[] {\n97\t  const source = readFileSync(file, 'utf8');\n98\t  const lines = source.split(/\\r?\\n/);\n99\t  const operations: ResolverOperation[] = [];\n100\t\n101\t  let pendingRoles: string[] | null = null;\n102\t  let pendingKind: 'Mutation' | 'Query' | null = null;\n103\t  let pendingDecoratorLine = 0;\n104\t  let pendingNameOverride: string | null = null;\n105\t\n106\t  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {\n107\t    const line = lines[i] ?? '';\n108\t\n109\t    // Track @Roles — only counts when it sits immediately above a\n110\t    // @Mutation/@Query. A @Roles above another decorator like\n111\t    // @Field simply lapses before it matches.\n112\t    const rolesMatch = ROLES_LINE_RE.exec(line);\n113\t    if (rolesMatch) {\n114\t      const raw = rolesMatch[1] ?? '';\n115\t      pendingRoles = Array.from(raw.matchAll(ROLE_TOKEN_RE)).map(\n116\t        (m) => m[1] ?? '',\n117\t      );\n118\t      continue;\n119\t    }\n120\t\n121\t    const kindMatch = MUTATION_OR_QUERY_LINE_RE.exec(line);\n122\t    if (kindMatch) {\n123\t      pendingKind = kindMatch[1] as 'Mutation' | 'Query';\n124\t      pendingDecoratorLine = i + 1;\n125\t      // The whole decorator call may span multiple lines. Scan\n126\t      // forward until the matching closing `)` — tracked by a\n127\t      // simple paren depth counter so the scanner doesn't stop\n128\t      // on the `()` in `() => SomeType` return-type arrows.\n129\t      // Ignores parens inside strings so `name: 'foo(bar)'` would\n130\t      // still close correctly; strings are walked char-by-char.\n131\t      let joined = '';\n132\t      let depth = 0;\n133\t      let closed = false;\n134\t      let j = i;\n135\t      while (j < lines.length && !closed) {\n136\t        const current = lines[j] ?? '';\n137\t        joined += (j === i ? [...pendingRoles].sort() : [],\n188\t        filePath: file,\n189\t        line: pendingDecoratorLine,\n190\t      });\n191\t      pendingRoles = null;\n192\t      pendingKind = null;\n193\t      pendingNameOverride = null;\n194\t    }\n195\t  }\n196\t\n197\t  return operations;\n198\t}\n199\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9cdaf3f8-2b4f-4f3f-95af-eef580e7197a","uuid":"c3fa96b0-7a3f-4fff-8818-778a6bb8b476","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:07:00.655Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-b9d9a4f17928/apps/farm-service/src/common/authz/resolver-scanner.ts","content":"/**\n * Resolver Scanner\n *\n * Walks every `*.resolver.ts` under `apps/farm-service/src/` and\n * extracts each root @Mutation / @Query's authorisation intent:\n *\n *   { operation: string, kind: 'Mutation'|'Query', roles: string[] }\n *\n * The scanner is regex-driven (not a full TypeScript AST) because\n * the @Roles / @Mutation / @Query decorator pattern in this codebase\n * is stylised: the decorator is always on its own line, the method\n * declaration that follows is always `async <name>(` or `<name>(`,\n * and role names are always `Role.XYZ`. The regex is anchored on\n * those conventions, and the invariant test fails loudly if the\n * pattern ever drifts — which is the safest way to catch a bad\n * decorator stack that might silently open up a mutation.\n *\n * Used by:\n *   - `permission-matrix.ts` at design time to snapshot the intent\n *     of the codebase into a single source of truth.\n *   - `permission-matrix.spec.ts` invariant test to guarantee the\n *     matrix stays in sync with the resolvers on every PR.\n *\n * Phase 6.1 of the \"Farm modülü kalan kör noktalar\" plan. Closes\n * Girdi 15-C2.\n */\nimport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'fs';\nimport { join, resolve } from 'path';\n\nexport interface ResolverOperation {\n  /** Top-level GraphQL operation name (method name by default). */\n  operation: string;\n  kind: 'Mutation' | 'Query';\n  /** Bare role tokens as written in the decorator (without the Role. prefix). */\n  roles: string[];\n  /** Absolute file path so test output can cite the offending line. */\n  filePath: string;\n  /** Class member line number (1-based). */\n  line: number;\n}\n\nconst DEFAULT_FARM_SERVICE_ROOT = resolve(\n  __dirname,\n  '..',\n  '..',\n  '..',\n);\n\n/**\n * Produce the sorted list of every root-level @Mutation / @Query in\n * the given farm-service source tree. When `rootDir` is omitted we\n * walk the tree relative to this file's compiled location, which\n * resolves to `apps/farm-service/src` in both dev and Jest runs.\n */\nexport function scanResolvers(rootDir: string = DEFAULT_FARM_SERVICE_ROOT): ResolverOperation[] {\n  const resolverFiles = collectResolverFiles(rootDir);\n  const ops: ResolverOperation[] = [];\n  for (const file of resolverFiles) {\n    ops.push(...extractOperationsFromFile(file));\n  }\n  ops.sort((a, b) => {\n    if (a.kind !== b.kind) return a.kind === 'Mutation' ? -1 : 1;\n    return a.operation.localeCompare(b.operation);\n  });\n  return ops;\n}\n\nfunction collectResolverFiles(dir: string, acc: string[] = []): string[] {\n  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {\n    const full = join(dir, entry);\n    const stat = statSync(full);\n    if (stat.isDirectory()) {\n      if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === '__tests__' || entry === 'dist') {\n        continue;\n      }\n      collectResolverFiles(full, acc);\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (stat.isFile() && entry.endsWith('.resolver.ts') && !entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) {\n      acc.push(full);\n    }\n  }\n  return acc;\n}\n\nconst ROLES_LINE_RE =\n  /^\\s*@Roles\\(\\s*([^)]*)\\)\\s*$/;\nconst MUTATION_OR_QUERY_LINE_RE =\n  /^\\s*@(Mutation|Query)\\(/;\nconst METHOD_SIG_RE =\n  /^\\s*(?:public\\s+|protected\\s+|private\\s+)?(?:async\\s+)?([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\s*[(<]/;\nconst ROLE_TOKEN_RE = /Role\\.([A-Z_]+)/g;\nconst NAME_OPTION_RE =\n  /name\\s*:\\s*['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]/;\n\nfunction extractOperationsFromFile(file: string): ResolverOperation[] {\n  const source = readFileSync(file, 'utf8');\n  const lines = source.split(/\\r?\\n/);\n  const operations: ResolverOperation[] = [];\n\n  let pendingRoles: string[] | null = null;\n  let pendingKind: 'Mutation' | 'Query' | null = null;\n  let pendingDecoratorLine = 0;\n  let pendingNameOverride: string | null = null;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {\n    const line = lines[i] ?? '';\n\n    // Track @Roles — only counts when it sits immediately above a\n    // @Mutation/@Query. A @Roles above another decorator like\n    // @Field simply lapses before it matches.\n    const rolesMatch = ROLES_LINE_RE.exec(line);\n    if (rolesMatch) {\n      const raw = rolesMatch[1] ?? '';\n      pendingRoles = Array.from(raw.matchAll(ROLE_TOKEN_RE)).map(\n        (m) => m[1] ?? '',\n      );\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const kindMatch = MUTATION_OR_QUERY_LINE_RE.exec(line);\n    if (kindMatch) {\n      pendingKind = kindMatch[1] as 'Mutation' | 'Query';\n      pendingDecoratorLine = i + 1;\n      // The whole decorator call may span multiple lines. Scan\n      // forward until the matching closing `)` — tracked by a\n      // simple paren depth counter so the scanner doesn't stop\n      // on the `()` in `() => SomeType` return-type arrows.\n      // Ignores parens inside strings so `name: 'foo(bar)'` would\n      // still close correctly; strings are walked char-by-char.\n      let joined = '';\n      let depth = 0;\n      let closed = false;\n      let j = i;\n      while (j < lines.length && !closed) {\n        const current = lines[j] ?? '';\n        joined += (j === i ? '' : '\\n') + current;\n        let inStr: '\"' | \"'\" | '`' | null = null;\n        let escaped = false;\n        for (const ch of current) {\n          if (escaped) {\n            escaped = false;\n            continue;\n          }\n          if (inStr) {\n            if (ch === '\\\\') {\n              escaped = true;\n            } else if (ch === inStr) {\n              inStr = null;\n            }\n            continue;\n          }\n          if (ch === '\"' || ch === \"'\" || ch === '`') {\n            inStr = ch;\n            continue;\n          }\n          if (ch === '(') {\n            depth += 1;\n          } else if (ch === ')') {\n            depth -= 1;\n            if (depth === 0) {\n              closed = true;\n              break;\n            }\n          }\n        }\n        j += 1;\n      }\n      const nameMatch = NAME_OPTION_RE.exec(joined);\n      pendingNameOverride = nameMatch?.[1] ?? null;\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const sigMatch = METHOD_SIG_RE.exec(line);\n    if (sigMatch && pendingKind) {\n      const methodName = sigMatch[1] ?? '';\n      if (!methodName || methodName === 'constructor') {\n        pendingRoles = null;\n        pendingKind = null;\n        pendingNameOverride = null;\n        continue;\n      }\n      const operation = pendingNameOverride ?? methodName;\n      operations.push({\n        operation,\n        kind: pendingKind,\n        roles: pendingRoles ? [...pendingRoles].sort() : [],\n        filePath: file,\n        line: pendingDecoratorLine,\n      });\n      pendingRoles = null;\n      pendingKind = null;\n      pendingNameOverride = null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return operations;\n}\n","numLines":199,"startLine":1,"totalLines":199}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013RPknfQPPjN17uqzDEHUsV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * INVARIANT — no dead guards (ADR-0010, SEC-HIGH-060).\n3\t *\n4\t * `IpWhitelistGuard` sat in gateway-api for two years registered in no\n5\t * module, under no `APP_GUARD`, in no `@UseGuards()`. It read as an access\n6\t * control and controlled nothing. A guard class that exists but is wired to\n7\t * no request path is worse than no guard: reviewers, auditors and the next\n8\t * engineer all read it as protection.\n9\t *\n10\t * Rule: every non-abstract class implementing `CanActivate` under `apps/**`\n11\t * and `libs/**` (production source, not tests) must be reachable from a\n12\t * registration site:\n13\t *   - an `APP_GUARD` provider (`useClass`, `useExisting`, or a `useFactory`\n14\t *     that constructs it),\n15\t *   - a `@UseGuards(...)` / `UseGuards(...)` call (controller, resolver,\n16\t *     handler, or an `applyDecorators` bundle),\n17\t *   - a `globalGuards: [...]` / `useGlobalGuards(...)` bootstrap list,\n18\t *   - or a registered guard that `extends` it,\n19\t * or carry an entry in `.claude/allowlists/unregistered-guards.yaml` with\n20\t * `{owner, expiry, findingId, reason}`. Expired entries fail; entries whose\n21\t * class no longer exists fail (the list only shrinks).\n22\t *\n23\t * The second block keeps both retired IP access-rule stacks deleted.\n24\t */\n25\t\n26\timport { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';\n27\timport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\n28\timport { resolve } from 'node:path';\n29\t\n30\timport * as yaml from 'js-yaml';\n31\t\n32\tconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n33\tconst ALLOWLIST = resolve(REPO_ROOT, '.claude/allowlists/unregistered-guards.yaml');\n34\t\n35\t/** Production source only: tests, fixtures and built output are not registration evidence. */\n36\tconst NOT_PRODUCTION =\n37\t  /(^|\\/)(__tests__|__mocks__|test|tests|e2e|dist|\\.archive)\\/|\\.(spec|test)\\.tsx?$/;\n38\t\n39\tfunction listSourceFiles(): string[] {\n40\t  return execFileSync(\n41\t    'git',\n42\t    [\n43\t      '-C',\n44\t      REPO_ROOT,\n45\t      'ls-files',\n46\t      '--cached',\n47\t      '--others',\n48\t      '--exclude-standard',\n49\t      '--',\n50\t      'apps',\n51\t      'libs',\n52\t      'platform',\n53\t    ],\n54\t    { encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 },\n55\t  )\n56\t    .split('\\n')\n57\t    .filter((path) => path.endsWith('.ts') && !NOT_PRODUCTION.test(path));\n58\t}\n59\t\n60\t/** Drop block + line comments so docstrings do not register as code. */"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9cdaf3f8-2b4f-4f3f-95af-eef580e7197a","uuid":"701c4980-feef-42ff-8920-107685097b64","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:07:52.515Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-b9d9a4f17928/tests/invariants/no-dead-guards.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * INVARIANT — no dead guards (ADR-0010, SEC-HIGH-060).\n *\n * `IpWhitelistGuard` sat in gateway-api for two years registered in no\n * module, under no `APP_GUARD`, in no `@UseGuards()`. It read as an access\n * control and controlled nothing. A guard class that exists but is wired to\n * no request path is worse than no guard: reviewers, auditors and the next\n * engineer all read it as protection.\n *\n * Rule: every non-abstract class implementing `CanActivate` under `apps/**`\n * and `libs/**` (production source, not tests) must be reachable from a\n * registration site:\n *   - an `APP_GUARD` provider (`useClass`, `useExisting`, or a `useFactory`\n *     that constructs it),\n *   - a `@UseGuards(...)` / `UseGuards(...)` call (controller, resolver,\n *     handler, or an `applyDecorators` bundle),\n *   - a `globalGuards: [...]` / `useGlobalGuards(...)` bootstrap list,\n *   - or a registered guard that `extends` it,\n * or carry an entry in `.claude/allowlists/unregistered-guards.yaml` with\n * `{owner, expiry, findingId, reason}`. Expired entries fail; entries whose\n * class no longer exists fail (the list only shrinks).\n *\n * The second block keeps both retired IP access-rule stacks deleted.\n */\n\nimport { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nimport * as yaml from 'js-yaml';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\nconst ALLOWLIST = resolve(REPO_ROOT, '.claude/allowlists/unregistered-guards.yaml');\n\n/** Production source only: tests, fixtures and built output are not registration evidence. */\nconst NOT_PRODUCTION =\n  /(^|\\/)(__tests__|__mocks__|test|tests|e2e|dist|\\.archive)\\/|\\.(spec|test)\\.tsx?$/;\n\nfunction listSourceFiles(): string[] {\n  return execFileSync(\n    'git',\n    [\n      '-C',\n      REPO_ROOT,\n      'ls-files',\n      '--cached',\n      '--others',\n      '--exclude-standard',\n      '--',\n      'apps',\n      'libs',\n      'platform',\n    ],\n    { encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 },\n  )\n    .split('\\n')\n    .filter((path) => path.endsWith('.ts') && !NOT_PRODUCTION.test(path));\n}\n\n/** Drop block + line comments so docstrings do not register as code. */","numLines":60,"startLine":1,"totalLines":333}}}

Reuse the\n547\t    // shared distributed sliding-window implementation so every direct GraphQL\n548\t    // request is bounded, including the expensive environment read surface.\n549\t    {\n550\t      provide: APP_GUARD,\n551\t      useFactory: (\n552\t        reflector: Reflector,\n553\t        configService: ConfigService,\n554\t        rateLimiter: SlidingWindowStrategy,\n555\t      ): ThrottlerGuard => new ThrottlerGuard(reflector, configService, rateLimiter),\n556\t      inject: [Reflector, ConfigService, SlidingWindowStrategy],\n557\t    },\n558\t    // Phase 6.1.2 — fail-closed permission-matrix guard. Rejects\n559\t    // any GraphQL @Mutation / @Query whose operation name is not\n560\t    // present in permission-matrix.ts. Grandfathered operations\n561\t    // (UNGATED_OPERATIONS) pass; unknown ones return 403. This is\n562\t    // the runtime counterpart to the build-time invariant test —\n563\t    // together they make \"new mutation without matrix entry\" a\n564\t    // zero-time-to-detect regression.\n565\t    {\n566\t      provide: APP_GUARD,\n567\t      useClass: PermissionMatrixGuard,\n568\t    },\n569\t    // Bootstrap source schema tables on startup (creates template tables if missing)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9cdaf3f8-2b4f-4f3f-95af-eef580e7197a","uuid":"d4440e38-62a2-422e-9674-502fccccc93d","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:08:43.575Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-b9d9a4f17928/apps/farm-service/src/app.module.ts","content":"      useClass: FarmAppErrorFilter,\n    },\n    // SECURITY: Service identity guard - validates HMAC-signed service identity headers\n    // Must be FIRST guard (before tenant/roles) to verify request origin\n    // WHY: useFactory bypasses reflect-metadata resolution which fails in Docker Alpine.\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService): ServiceIdentityGuard =>\n        new ServiceIdentityGuard(configService, undefined, 'farm-service'),\n      inject: [ConfigService],\n    },\n    // Tenant guard\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useFactory: (\n        reflector: Reflector,\n        auditLogService: IAuditLogService,\n        configService: ConfigService,\n      ): TenantGuard => new TenantGuard(reflector, auditLogService, configService),\n      inject: [Reflector, AUDIT_LOG_SERVICE, ConfigService],\n    },\n    // SECURITY: Roles guard - enforces @Roles() decorator authorization\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useFactory: (reflector: Reflector): RolesGuard => new RolesGuard(reflector),\n      inject: [Reflector],\n    },\n    // Direct-subgraph defense: gateway rate limits are not an authority\n    // boundary when a deployment accidentally exposes farm-service. Reuse the\n    // shared distributed sliding-window implementation so every direct GraphQL\n    // request is bounded, including the expensive environment read surface.\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useFactory: (\n        reflector: Reflector,\n        configService: ConfigService,\n        rateLimiter: SlidingWindowStrategy,\n      ): ThrottlerGuard => new ThrottlerGuard(reflector, configService, rateLimiter),\n      inject: [Reflector, ConfigService, SlidingWindowStrategy],\n    },\n    // Phase 6.1.2 — fail-closed permission-matrix guard. Rejects\n    // any GraphQL @Mutation / @Query whose operation name is not\n    // present in permission-matrix.ts. Grandfathered operations\n    // (UNGATED_OPERATIONS) pass; unknown ones return 403. This is\n    // the runtime counterpart to the build-time invariant test —\n    // together they make \"new mutation without matrix entry\" a\n    // zero-time-to-detect regression.\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useClass: PermissionMatrixGuard,\n    },\n    // Bootstrap source schema tables on startup (creates template tables if missing)","numLines":52,"startLine":518,"totalLines":606}}}

\"aria-evidence-judge\",\n      \"model\": \"claude-opus-5\",\n      \"prompt_hash\": null,\n      \"confidence\": 0.78,\n      \"rationale\": \"Adapter claim verified both literally and on substance. (1) Security-sensitive: the resolver imports TenantGuard and MobileFeatureGuard (harvest.resolver.ts:10), applies @UseGuards(TenantGuard, MobileFeatureGuard) at class level (:197), carries per-operation @Roles floors (:213, :266, :281, :308, :353, :378, :412), gates createHarvestRecord with @RequiresMobileFeature('harvest') (:354), and threads the caller's roles and assignedSiteIds from @CurrentUser (:357) into CreateHarvestRecordCommand (:367, :368) - the SEC-HIGH-051 object-level site-authz input. (2) No adjacent or importing test: apps/farm-service/src/harvest/resolvers/ contains only harvest.resolver.ts and harvest-plan.resolver.ts (no __tests__ directory, no *.spec.ts); a repo-wide grep for HarvestResolver returns only the file itself and the module wiring at harvest.module.ts:55 and :110; a grep for HarvestResolver or harvest.resolver across every *.spec.ts / *.test.ts / *.e2e-spec.ts returns nothing; operation-name hits in tests (createHarvestRecord and siblings) are comments or metric/registry string literals inside other units' specs, not resolver exercise. (3) Boundary the fix must respect: the @Roles floors ARE already pinned by a structural security test that the adapter's adjacent-or-importing heuristic cannot see - permission-matrix.spec.ts:57 calls scanResolvers, resolver-scanner.ts:79 walks every *.resolver.ts under farm-service, permission-matrix.spec.ts:98 asserts source decorators equal the matrix, and permission-matrix.ts carries explicit entries for all seven harvest operations (:84 createHarvestRecord, :113 deleteHarvestRecord, :251 updateHarvestRecord, :387 harvest, :396 harvestStatistics, :397 harvests, :398 harvestsByBatch) that match the decorators in source. TenantGuard, RolesGuard and PermissionMatrixGuard are also global APP_GUARDs (app.module.ts:531, :541, :566), so the tenant and role floors have a runtime counterpart. (4) What remains untested at every layer and decides true_positive: MobileFeatureGuard is NOT a global guard - its only enforcement on the harvest surface is the class-level @UseGuards at :197 (provider registered at harvest.module.ts:92) together with the :354 decorator, and no test in the repository asserts either one on this resolver (the only RequiresMobileFeature/MobileFeatureGuard tests are the generic guard spec in libs/backend-common and unrelated weather/gateway specs). Deleting :197's MobileFeatureGuard or :354 silently disables the SEC-HIGH-052 harvest entitlement gate with a green test suite. Likewise the pass-through of user.roles and user.assignedSiteIds (:367, :368) has no resolver-level test: create-harvest-record.handler.spec.ts:211 exercises the handler's site authz GIVEN those inputs, not that the resolver supplies them. Recommended root-cause fix (architectural tier 3, make it detectable): add apps/farm-service/src/harvest/resolvers/__tests__/harvest.resolver.spec.ts that (a) reads the NestJS guard metadata on HarvestResolver and the mobile-feature metadata on createHarvestRecord via Reflector and asserts MobileFeatureGuard and the 'harvest' feature key are present, and (b) instantiates HarvestResolver with a mocked CommandBus and asserts createHarvestRecord dispatches a CreateHarvestRecordCommand carrying user.roles and user.assignedSiteIds from the @CurrentUser context. (1) Security-sensitive: the resolver imports TenantGuard and MobileFeatureGuard (harvest.resolver.ts:10), applies @UseGuards(TenantGuard, MobileFeatureGuard) at class level (:197), carries per-operation @Roles floors (:213, :266, :281, :308, :353, :378, :412), gates createHarvestRecord with @RequiresMobileFeature('harvest') (:354), and threads the caller's roles and assignedSiteIds from @CurrentUser (:357) into CreateHarvestRecordCommand (:367, :368) - the SEC-HIGH-051 object-level site-authz input. (2) No adjacent or importing test: apps/farm-service/src/harvest/resolvers/ contains only harvest.resolver.ts and harvest-plan.resolver.ts (no __tests__ directory, no *.spec.ts); a repo-wide grep for HarvestResolver returns only the file itself and the module wiring at harvest.module.ts:55 and :110; a grep for HarvestResolver or harvest.resolver across every *.spec.ts / *.test.ts / *.e2e-spec.ts returns nothing; operation-name hits in tests (createHarvestRecord and siblings) are comments or metric/registry string literals inside other units' specs, not resolver exercise. (3) Boundary the fix must respect: the @Roles floors ARE already pinned by a structural security test that the adapter's adjacent-or-importing heuristic cannot see - permission-matrix.spec.ts:57 calls scanResolvers, resolver-scanner.ts:79 walks every *.resolver.ts under farm-service, permission-matrix.spec.ts:98 asserts source decorators equal the matrix, and permission-matrix.ts carries explicit entries for all seven harvest operations (:84 createHarvestRecord, :113 deleteHarvestRecord, :251 updateHarvestRecord, :387 harvest, :396 harvestStatistics, :397 harvests, :398 harvestsByBatch) that match the decorators in source. TenantGuard, RolesGuard and PermissionMatrixGuard are also global APP_GUARDs (app.module.ts:531, :541, :566), so the tenant and role floors have a runtime counterpart. (4) What remains untested at every layer and decides true_positive: MobileFeatureGuard is NOT a global guard - its only enforcement on the harvest surface is the class-level @UseGuards at :197 (provider registered at harvest.module.ts:92) together with the :354 decorator, and no test in the repository asserts either one on this resolver (the only RequiresMobileFeature/MobileFeatureGuard tests are the generic guard spec in libs/backend-common and unrelated weather/gateway specs). Deleting :197's MobileFeatureGuard or :354 silently disables the SEC-HIGH-052 harvest entitlement gate with a green test suite. Likewise the pass-through of user.roles and user.assignedSiteIds (:367, :368) has no resolver-level test: create-harvest-record.handler.spec.ts:211 exercises the handler's site authz GIVEN those inputs, not that the resolver supplies them. Recommended root-cause fix (architectural tier 3, make it detectable): add apps/farm-service/src/harvest/resolvers/__tests__/harvest.resolver.spec.ts that (a) reads the NestJS guard metadata on HarvestResolver and the mobile-feature metadata on createHarvestRecord via Reflector and asserts MobileFeatureGuard and the 'harvest' feature key are present, and (b) instantiates HarvestResolver with a mocked CommandBus and asserts createHarvestRecord dispatches a CreateHarvestRecordCommand carrying user.roles and user.assignedSiteIds from the @CurrentUser context. Do not re-test the @Roles floors (permission-matrix.spec.ts owns them) and do not close the finding through the adapter allowlist at test-gap-adapter.ts:150, which is a tier-4 escape hatch.
